<PARSED TEXT FOR SHEET: 22 / 22 TABS
TAB NAME: data_notes>
index,note_id,table_name,description
0,N001,players,Player IDs use exact source names. Abbreviations and spelling variants are retained as distinct records; identity has not been independently verified.
1,N002,trade_assets,"from_items are assets sent from the trade from-team to its to-team; to_items flow in the opposite direction, matching the website rendering. Contract years are unavailable in the source and remain blank."
2,N003,trades,"Includes website allTradeRows records, with vetoed and COVID-reversed status preserved. Blank executed_explicit means the source did not supply that flag. Recorded does not independently verify execution."
3,N004,sources,Export retrieved from ecfhl.win live assets on 2026-09-24. Source embedded as_of date is older and does not represent export retrieval date.
4,N005,team_seasons,"Regular-season rank is distinct from final playoff placing, which is stored in awards. Missing statistics remain blank."
5,N006,playoff_games,Only structured live-site bracket records exported. Blank scores remain unknown. Tied scores do not infer a winner. 2025-26 Loser Pool early-round matchups are absent in the source.
6,N007,prize_awards,"Snapshot of the live website prize calculation, stored in integer cents. No additional financial facts were inferred."
7,N008,franchise_aliases,Uses current aliases only. Source also retains a pending Les Pousseux D'Puck mapping conflict; the current franchise list treats that team separately.
8,N009,draft_picks,All selections in the live draft-picks.json are included. Missing seasons/rounds were not invented.
